### [1] [Ephemeral Feeds and Enduring Rituals: RushTok and the Formation of Event-Based Algorithmic Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09331)
*Emelia Hughes,Tim Weninger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文以TikTok上的“RushTok”现象为对象，将其视为围绕特定线下仪式（阿拉巴马大学姐妹会招募）形成的、由推荐算法维系的“事件型算法社区”，探讨其参与者构成、参与方式与参与意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年8月，TikTok的“For You”页面会把阿拉巴马大学的姐妹会招募变成RushTok。作者关注一个由算法推荐聚合而成、围绕有限时段线下仪式展开的社群，试图理解这种新型社区的边界、归属与治理问题。

Method: 采用混合方法：一项问卷调查（n=71）以及对创作者外联过程的反思性记录（reflexive account）。调查考察谁参与、如何参与及参与的意义；外联部分记录联系创作者进行访谈的过程与结果。

Result: 发现一条模糊且以娱乐为基础的贯穿线索；51/71的人称其为“社区”，但仅11/71自称是其成员。归属感围绕创作者而非共同实践形成，准社会关注集中在少数潜在新成员（PNMs）和回归人物身上。内容接触越多，越倾向于自我认同为社区成员；这些成员会评论、关注创作者并跨视频参与。联系创作者进行访谈的努力遭遇沉默或拒绝，体现出即便具有病毒式可见度，仍存在社区边界工作。

Conclusion: 作者提出对平台治理的启示，包括时间限定的语境、渐进式可见性和事后关怀（aftercare）。

Abstract: Each August, TikTok's For You page turns the University of Alabama's sorority recruitment into RushTok. We examine RushTok as an event-based algorithmic community: a collective assembled around a bounded offline ritual and sustained by recommendation. Using a mixed-methods survey (n=71) and a reflexive account of creator outreach, we ask who participates, how, and with what stakes. Findings show an ambiguous and entertainment based throughline; many called it a community (51/71) but few claimed membership (11/71). Affiliation centered on creators rather than shared practices, with parasocial attention clustering around a small set of potential new members (PNMs) and returning figures. Higher content exposure tracked with self-identification as a community member; those members commented, followed creators, and engaged across videos. Attempts to interview creators were met with silence or refusals, reflecting community boundary-work despite viral visibility. We outline implications for platform governance, including time-bounded context, graduated visibility, and aftercare.

</details>


### [2] [Early Epistemic Settlement in AI-Assisted Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09332)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"早期认识settlement"概念，指出语言模型在写作者自身建构尚未完成时即可解决其当前组织问题，从而可能取代具有生成性的写作工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注语言模型辅助写作的一个被忽视风险：模型提供的组织方案虽满足当下写作要求，却可能使写作者免于进行本可改变其写作要求、或使其具备形成新组织能力的关键建构工作。

Method: 概念分析。区分"完成已可形成之组织所需的协调"与"改变哪些组织可被形成的建构"两类工作，并据此分析settlement在两类情形中的作用差异。

Result: 在第一类情形中，settlement改变将可用组织推进至充分状态所需的相对工作量；在第二类情形中，它可能直接移除使另一组织变得可形成所需的工作。即便模型方案与继续建构呈现相同的可见合格结果，写作者探究中不同的依赖关系仍可能支撑不同的后续组织。

Conclusion: 在理论与探索性写作中，达成局部充分性的过程中所发展的关系，决定了写作者日后能够辩护或展开的内容；因此对当前段落"足够"的正确判断，可能在生成性工作发生之前就使进一步探究被误认为不必要。

Abstract: A language model can resolve a writer's current organizing problem while the construction needed for her own resolution remains unfinished. I call this early epistemic settlement. The supplied organization meets every demand then governing the passage, yet proceeding from it can displace work through which the writer would have changed those demands or become able to form further organizations. I distinguish the coordination needed to complete an already formable organization from construction that changes which organizations are formable in the first place. Settlement in the first case changes the relative work still required to bring available organizations to sufficiency. In the second, it can remove the need for the work through which another organization would become formable. Even when supplied resolution and continued construction leave the same visible qualification, different dependencies in the writer's inquiry may support different later organizations. Model suggestions can also contribute to this development when writers work through them while the problem remains unresolved. In theoretical and exploratory writing, the relations developed in reaching local adequacy help determine what the writer can later defend or develop. A sound judgment that the present passage is sufficient can therefore make further inquiry dispensable before that generative work has occurred.

</details>


### [3] [Where Does the Human End? Creative Agency with Generative AI across Five Years of Chinese Digital Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09333)
*Yibo Meng,Ruiqi Chen,Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Chengxi Zang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过五年纵向访谈研究，追踪17位中国数字画家人机交互中的角色与能动性边界变迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI进入创意工作，从业者需界定AI辅助与人类创作的边界，而现有HAI研究多为横断面，缺乏对AI角色随能力、公共性和经济嵌入度演变的纵向考察。

Method: 对17位中国数字画家进行2021至2025年共五年的年度半结构化访谈，分析其保护性抵抗、务实任务委派与反思性能动性重新分配的模式。

Result: 早期抵抗保护观察、原创性、署名与所有权；后期委派局限于参考、背景、草图、客户初稿等有限任务；2025年部分人建立含"人类专属区"的混合流程，另一些人则疲劳、不稳定或难以定位人类角色；同伴规范、情感氛围与生产压力影响委派的感受；版权、作者身份与创意劳动构成持续限制。

Conclusion: 提出"纵向能动性分区"框架，描述在创意人机交互中决定哪些阶段、责任、价值与主张保持为人类的情境化工作，并为可修订的能动性边界控制、溯源脚手架与社群作者规范提供设计启示。

Abstract: As generative AI enters creative work, practitioners must decide where AI assistance ends and human authorship begins. Human-agent interaction (HAI) research has examined AI as a tool, collaborator, consultant, and competitor. The longitudinal problem is how these roles are revised as systems become more capable, public, and economically embedded. We report a five-year interview study with 17 Chinese digital painters, based on annual semi-structured interviews from 2021 to 2025. Participants described recurring but non-uniform patterns of protective resistance, pragmatic task delegation, and, for some, reflective agency repartitioning. Early resistance protected observation, originality, signature, and ownership from AI. Later delegation placed AI in bounded tasks such as references, backgrounds, rough sketches, and client-facing drafts. By 2025, some participants built hybrid workflows around human-only zones, while others described fatigue, precarity, or difficulty locating a remaining human role. Peer norms, emotional climates, and production pressures shaped which delegations felt useful, acceptable, or exhausting. Copyright, authorship, and creative labor remained recurring limits on what participants were willing to delegate. We frame these accounts as longitudinal agency partitioning, the situated work of deciding which stages, responsibilities, values, and claims remain human in creative human-agent interaction. We discuss design implications for revisable agency-boundary controls, provenance scaffolds, and community-facing authorship norms.

</details>


### [4] [Echoes in the Algorithm: Analyzing the Fidelity of User Preferences Against Realized Platform Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09365)
*Emelia Hughes,Tim Weninger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过网页游戏 TokOrNot 让 363 名参与者对成对 TikTok 视频进行偏好判断与传播量判断，并与真实播放量比对，发现用户仅略高于随机水平地识别高传播视频，说明在缺乏显性流行度线索时用户难以从内容本身可靠推断平台成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台常隐藏、延迟或边缘化点赞数与播放量等流行度线索，但用户仍会形成关于哪些视频正在走红的印象。论文关注的问题是：当显性流行度信号缺失时，用户能否仅凭内容判断视频的实际传播范围，以及这种判断与个人偏好之间的关系。

Method: 研究设计了一个基于网页的游戏 TokOrNot，参与者比较成对的 TikTok 视频并报告两项内容：(i) 更喜欢哪一个；(ii) 认为哪一个获得了更大的受众。研究者以经过验证的公开播放量作为平台实际传播范围的有界代理指标，对上述判断进行基准比较。样本包含 363 名参与者的 3,513 次判断。

Result: 参与者识别高传播视频的正确率为 56.75%（95% CI: 56.01–58.55），仅小幅高于随机水平。偏好与高播放量视频的一致性比例相近，而偏好与传播量预测在 83.48% 的试次中相互匹配（95% CI: 83.12–85.95）。表现还因内容类别而异。

Conclusion: 结果并不表明用户能仅凭内容可靠地读出平台成功，而是指向一种更松散、更不确定的解释过程：在缺乏显性流行度线索时，传播量判断往往跟随个人品味或其他弱启发式。作者讨论了这对算法素养的启示，以及对减少可见指标但同时避免让用户仅凭不均衡或特异线索推断传播范围的界面设计的含义。

Abstract: What does popular content look like when platforms withhold the usual cues? On TikTok, users still form impressions about which videos are taking off even when likes and view counts are hidden, delayed, or pushed to the margins of the interface. We study this problem through TokOrNot, a web-based game in which participants compared pairs of TikTok videos and reported (i) which one they preferred and (ii) which one they believed had reached a larger audience. We benchmark these judgments against verified public view counts, which we use as a bounded proxy for realized platform reach. Across 3,513 judgments from 363 participants, participants identified the higher-reach video only modestly above chance (56.75%, 95% CI: 56.01-58.55). Preference aligned with the higher-view video at a similar rate, while preference and prediction matched in 83.48% of trials (95% CI: 83.12-85.95). Performance also varied across content categories. Taken together, these results do not suggest that users can reliably read platform success from content alone. Instead, they point to a looser and more uncertain interpretive process in which reach judgments often track personal taste or other weak heuristics when explicit popularity cues are absent. We discuss the implications for algorithmic literacy and for interface designs that reduce visible metrics without leaving users to infer reach from uneven or idiosyncratic cues alone.

</details>


### [5] [Agentic Web Accessibility Auditing: Authoring and Evaluating Per-Criterion Worker Agents for WCAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09379)
*Arjun Mishra,Pranav Karthik,Byungjun Bae,Dongwook Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个结合共享浏览器工具与准则特定worker代理的无障碍评估框架，实现39项WCAG 2.1 A/AA准则及1项WCAG 2.2准则，在学术平台专业审计记录上正例召回率达0.86，优于axe-core（0.36）和无提示视觉语言模型（0.67），但精确率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化无障碍评估工具在收集的证据和针对的需求上存在差异，缺乏统一、可检查的评估方式，且现有工具召回能力有限。

Method: 构建框架，将共享浏览器工具与准则特定worker代理相结合，实现39项WCAG 2.1 A/AA准则和1项WCAG 2.2准则；在来自学术平台专业审计的250条页面-准则记录上进行评估，并与axe-core、无提示视觉语言模型对比。

Result: worker恢复0.86的正例参考标签，axe-core为0.36，无提示视觉语言模型为0.67，但worker精确率较低；准则级结果、弃权情况、开发暴露敏感性和单独插桩的运行对比较结果有所限定。

Conclusion: 由于推断的负例标签及评估配置差异，关于真实准确率和因果效应的结论受限；论文贡献了框架、准则特定实现，以及区分检测、证据可用性和资源使用的评估说明，推动在专业审计中进一步研究可检查的自动化评估。

Abstract: Automated accessibility assessments differ in the evidence they collect and the requirements they address. We present a framework that combines shared browser tools with criterion-specific worker agents, implementing 39 WCAG 2.1 Level A and AA criteria and one additional WCAG 2.2 criterion. We analyze archived predictions on 250 page-criterion records derived from professional audits of scholarly platforms. Workers recover 0.86 of positive reference labels, compared with 0.36 for axe-core and 0.67 for an uncued vision-language model, with lower precision. Criterion-level results, abstentions, development-exposure sensitivities, and separately instrumented runs qualify these comparisons. Inferred negative labels and differences between evaluated configurations limit conclusions about true accuracy and causal effects. We contribute the framework, its criterion-specific implementation, and an evaluation account that distinguishes detection, evidence availability, and resource use, motivating further study of inspectable automated assessments within professional auditing.

</details>


### [6] ["It's Like Drinking from a Fire Hose": Understanding and Characterizing Video Learning Experiences for Individuals with ADHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09443)
*Hanxiu 'Hazel' Zhu,Weiyu Zhang,Ru Wang,Yuhang Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过眼动追踪与回溯性出声思维方法，访谈16名ADHD参与者观看视频讲座片段，揭示了阻碍其学习的视频设计元素、应对策略及其局限，并将行为信号与主观反思结合，刻画了这些体验的行为表现，最终提出面向ADHD友好自适应视频学习系统的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频讲座日益普及，但其静态画面、信息密集、时长较长，对ADHD人群构成注意力挑战。自适应学习虽为ADHD可及的视频学习提供机会，但如何恰当地适配仍不清楚：哪些多模态视频成分对ADHD观众有挑战？这些体验如何体现在行为信号中以触发适配？他们偏好何种呈现方式？

Method: 采用基于眼动追踪的回溯性出声思维研究，招募16名ADHD参与者，观看并反思一组精心挑选的视频讲座片段；联合分析行为信号与回溯性反思。

Result: 识别出阻碍学习的视频设计元素，揭示参与者的应对策略及其局限性；通过行为信号与反思的联合分析，刻画了这些体验在行为模式中的表现方式；并发现参与者在观看视频之外解决学习需求的实践。

Conclusion: 研究为未来面向ADHD的自适应视频学习系统提炼出设计启示，强调需兼顾视频设计元素、行为信号触发机制与学习者个性化偏好。

Abstract: Video lectures have become increasingly prevalent for education and professional development, yet their static visuals, dense information, and long duration pose attentional challenges for individuals with ADHD. While adaptive learning offers opportunities towards ADHD-accessible video learning, little is known about how to suitably adapt such videos: What components in multimodal video lectures are challenging for ADHD viewers? How do these experiences surface in behavioral signals to trigger an adaptation? What presentations do they prefer? To answer these questions, we conducted an eye-tracking-based retrospective think-aloud study with 16 participants with ADHD, who watched and reflected on a curated set of video lecture segments. Our study uncovered video design elements that hindered learning and revealed participants' coping strategies along with their limitations. By jointly analyzing behavioral signals and retrospective reflections, we characterized how these experiences manifested in behavioral patterns. We further surfaced participants' practices for addressing learning needs beyond the video watching process, and derived design implications for future ADHD-friendly adaptive video learning systems.

</details>


### [7] [Exploring 3D Glyph Physicalizations for Public Engagement through River Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09472)
*Maria Teresa Ortoleva,Min Chen,Rita Borgo,Alfie Abdul-Rahman*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文初步设计了一套基于图元的实体化（glyph-based physicalization）工具包，以伦敦河流健康数据为例，支持公众参与式的环境数据实体化制作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化相关的城市议题（如河流健康）数据复杂且多维，公众难以直观理解；作者希望借助实体化与公众参与，让公众在动手制作中理解数据、建立与自身经验的关联并产生关怀。

Method: 提出3D图元制作工具包的组件与编码方案，并以回收材料为介质，给出将河流水质数据逐步制作为实体化作品的操作流程；同时论述用户如何借模板学习数据集并完成自我映射与反思。

Result: 形成一套3D图元模板的组件、编码与制作流程设计，并从设计空间角度分析了其在支持公众参与、认知脚手架和自我反思方面的机会。

Conclusion: 未来该3D图元模板的实现将帮助不同能力的公众探索河流健康数据、实体化复杂信息并认识其现实意义，有望通过公众参与式工作坊提升意识、唤起关怀并培养归属感。

Abstract: Introduction: In this paper, we present the preliminary design of a toolkit for making glyph-based physicalizations for public engagement. We use London river health data as a case study: a data set of significance to urban issues related to climate change and of interest to draw public attention, as part of the Greater London Authority's strategies. Design: We present the components of a 3D glyph-making toolkit, its encodings, and a step-by-step process for crafting a physicalization of a river's water quality using recycled materials. We reason about how users can use the template to learn about a data set while reflecting on the data's significance to their personal experience and self-mapping onto the physicalization. Reflection: We reflect on the opportunities that extending the design space of glyphs to 3D physicalization offers for supporting public engagement with complex, multi-dimensional data sets, scaffolding cognitive processes, and self-reflection, thereby bringing crucial environmental data to life. Conclusion: Future implementation of the 3D glyph template will enable the public of all abilities to explore river health data, physicalize complexity, and realize its relevance. We hope that its use in public engagement workshops will help raise awareness, invite care, and foster a sense of belonging.

</details>


### [8] [Integrating Multi-Source Feedback in Computational Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09483)
*Francisco Erivaldo Fernandes Junior,Thomas Langerak,Mira Keränen,Danqing Shi,Ardak Alipova,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多源反馈整合方法并实现无代码工具MUSE，让设计师能组合并平衡专家意见、实证研究与计算模型等多种评估来源，实验显示该方法提升了设计师的信心与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实设计实践中，评估很少依赖单一判断来源。设计师通常需要结合专家意见、实证研究和计算模型，但每种来源都有各自的优势与局限。机器学习虽能整合多源反馈，但这些方法对缺乏技术专长的设计师而言难以使用，因此需要一种更易用的多源整合途径。

Method: 作者提出一种多源反馈整合的实用方法，并将其实现为无代码工具MUSE，使设计师能够组合和平衡多样化的评估来源。核心思路是对多个评估来源进行独立建模，从而支持异构反馈的探索、适应不同评估速度、揭示来源间的分歧，并支持可随时重配置的评估设置。研究通过可视化设计研究对方法进行验证。

Result: 技术层面，独立建模多评估来源实现了对异构反馈的探索、兼容不同评估速度、暴露来源间分歧，并支持设计师在过程中灵活重配置评估设置。在可视化设计研究中，参与者能够将自身判断与模拟器反馈结合使用，报告出更高的信心与灵活性感受。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了多源整合在支持计算设计方面的可行性，为弥合先进优化方法与设计实践之间的鸿沟迈出了一步。

Abstract: In real-world design practice, evaluations rarely rely on a single source of judgment. Designers routinely combine expert opinions, empirical studies, and computational models, each with distinct strengths and limitations. While machine learning offers methods to integrate multiple feedback sources, these approaches remain largely inaccessible to designers without technical expertise. In this paper, we explore how to integrate multiple feedback sources, primarily through: (1) a practical approach for multi-source integration, and (2) its implementation in MUSE, a no-code tool that allows designers to combine and balance diverse sources. Our technical findings show that independent modeling of multiple evaluation sources enables exploration across heterogeneous feedback, accommodates different evaluation speeds, surfaces disagreements between sources, and supports an adaptable evaluation setup that designers can reconfigure during their process. In a visualization design study, participants navigated their own judgments alongside simulator feedback, reporting a perception of enhanced confidence and flexibility. Our results highlight the viability of multi-source integration to support computational design, offering a step toward bridging the gap between advanced optimization methods and design practice.

</details>


### [9] [AppetiteCheck: Feasibility of Momentary Vagus Nerve Stimulation as an Implicit Intervention for Eating Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09700)
*Tan Gemicioglu,Jas Brooks,Pedro Lopes,Tanzeem Choudhury*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出经皮颈部迷走神经刺激（tcVNS）作为一种普适、低负担的进食干预手段，通过24人实验证明其可使进食量减少9.6%、进食速度减慢23.6%，为隐性生理干预界面提供了可行性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴食与情绪化进食是常见健康问题，甚至影响无进食障碍的人群。迷走神经在肠脑轴中起关键作用，植入式迷走神经刺激器已被证实与食欲下降相关。作者希望探索一种非侵入、可在进食过程中即时介入、且几乎不占用注意力的普适干预方式。

Method: 提出使用经皮颈部迷走神经刺激（tcVNS），并采用便携式手持tcVNS设备。在24名参与者中开展实验，在单次"分心吃零食"情境下对比迷走神经刺激与假刺激（sham）两种条件，测量进食量、进食速度、餐后饱腹感与心率，并收集主观感受描述。

Result: 与假刺激相比，迷走神经刺激条件下参与者进食量减少9.6%、进食速度减慢23.6%。两种条件下的餐后饱腹感相同，而迷走神经刺激期间心率更低。参与者将刺激描述为细微、几乎难以察觉。

Conclusion: 结果证明了非侵入式迷走神经刺激作为低注意力进食行为管理干预的可行性，并可将该能力封装进普适交互系统中。研究将隐性界面的设计空间拓展至生理干预方向，推动未来结合进食感知与低注意力干预的普适系统研究。

Abstract: Overeating and emotional eating are common health issues that affect people even without an eating disorder. The vagus nerve plays a critical role in the gut-brain axis, and implanted vagus nerve stimulators have been associated with reduced appetite. In this paper, we propose transcutaneous cervical vagus nerve stimulation (tcVNS) as a ubiquitous system to provide immediate, low-effort intervention during an eating episode. In a study with 24 participants, we evaluated a mobile, handheld tcVNS device during a single episode of distracted snacking. We found that participants ate 9.6% less and 23.6% more slowly during vagus nerve stimulation than during sham stimulation. Post-snacking satiety was the same in both conditions, while heart rate was lower during vagus nerve stimulation. The stimulation was described as subtle and barely noticeable. Overall, these results provide evidence for the feasibility of non-invasive vagus nerve stimulation as a low-attention intervention for managing eating behavior -- one that can be packaged inside ubiquitous interactive systems. As such, we extend the design space of implicit interfaces toward physiological intervention, motivating future ubiquitous systems that pair eating-related sensing with low-attention interventions.

</details>


### [10] [How Far Do Capability Cues Travel? Anthropomorphism and Differentiated Trust in a Platform-Embedded AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09713)
*Chenchen Mao,Hanjing Shi,Haiyan Jia,Dominic DiFranzo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项随机实验发现，界面展示AI功能数量只提升“多功能性感知”，并不直接转化为智力与信任判断；感知智力与拟人化等关系解释了信任分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者想检验一个常见假设：让AI能力“看得见”（展示功能、给出理由）是否会提升用户对AI智力与可信度的判断，并厘清界面效应与用户感知之间的关系。

Method: 采用2×2随机实验，270名美国Reddit用户。嵌入式助手展示1项或3项功能，并配或不配简短理由说明。测量六项结果变量，含感知多功能性、感知智力、拟人化及信任的能力、正直、善意三个维度，并做多重比较校正；另做探索性分析考察客观AI素养的调节作用。

Result: 展示3项功能提升感知多功能性，其余随机主效应经六项结果校正后均不显著；理由说明未可靠提升感知智力。探索性分析显示AI素养越高，功能展示的效果越强。相关分析中，感知多功能性与感知智力相关，感知智力与拟人化及三个信任维度均相关；控制感知智力后，拟人化仅与善意正相关，与正直、能力关系不稳定。

Conclusion: 可见能力不等于可信：界面设计主要影响功能感知，信任判断更多经由感知智力与拟人化的关联路径形成。能力、正直、善意应作为独立维度分别评估，不宜合并为单一“信任”指标。

Abstract: Visible AI capabilities need not translate into broader judgments of trustworthiness. In a randomized 2 x 2 experiment with 270 U.S.-based Reddit users, an embedded assistant displayed one or three functions, with or without a brief rationale. Displaying three functions increased perceived multifunctionality; no other randomized main effect survived correction across the six outcomes. Rationale availability did not reliably increase perceived intelligence. Exploratory analysis indicated stronger uptake of the functional display at higher objective AI literacy. Among concurrently measured judgments, perceived multifunctionality was associated with perceived intelligence, which was associated with anthropomorphism and all three trust dimensions. After accounting for perceived intelligence, anthropomorphism was positively associated with benevolence, but not reliably with integrity or ability. These findings separate interface effects from relationships among users' perceptions and show why ability, integrity, and benevolence should be evaluated separately.

</details>


### [11] [Pairit: A Platform for Live Experiments on Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09789)
*Harang Ju,Sinan Aral*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Pairit的在线平台，通过单一YAML配置文件来声明可执行的实验流程，支持在实时会话中混合任意数量的人类与AI智能体，用于研究人机组织的协调、委托与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能时代进行组织设计，需要能够测试人机群体如何协调、委托和决策的实验方法。现有的可编程平台可以协调实时的人-人会话或实时人机聊天，但研究者难以在一个可审计的统一配置中，声明让AI参与者既进行交流又对共享工作采取行动的实验协议。

Method: 提出Pairit在线平台，用于设计、测试和部署检验人机组织设计与干预的实验。通过单个YAML配置文件，研究者可声明可执行的实验图，包含页面、路由、随机化、匹配、聊天、共享工作区、服务器托管智能体、问卷调查、计时器和自定义HTML组件，并在实时会话中组合任意数量的人类与AI智能体。

Result: 通过多次实际部署（包括已同行评审发表的研究）验证了平台可行性，捕获了实时人机二元组在沟通、协商与协作工作中的高分辨率过程轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过将复杂的交互协议表示为标准化、可审计的配置文件，Pairit为指定、部署和分享实时人机组织实验提供了可复用的基础设施。

Abstract: Organizational design in the era of artificial intelligence requires experimental methods that can test how human-AI groups coordinate, delegate, and make decisions. Programmable platforms coordinate live human-to-human sessions or real-time human-AI chat, but researchers cannot easily declare experiment protocols in which AI participants both communicate and act on shared work within one auditable configuration. Here we introduce Pairit, an online platform that facilitates the design, testing, and deployment of experiments that test human-AI organizational designs and interventions. Through a single YAML configuration file, researchers declare an executable experiment graph (pages, routing, randomization, matchmaking, chat, shared workspaces, server-hosted agents, surveys, timers, and custom HTML components) and combine any number of humans and AI agents in live sessions. We have validated the feasibility of the platform through multiple live deployments, including peer-reviewed published studies, capturing high-resolution process traces of communication, negotiation, and collaborative work in live human-AI dyads. By representing complex interactive protocols as standardized, auditable configuration files, Pairit provides reusable infrastructure for specifying, deploying, and sharing live human-AI organizational experiments.

</details>


### [12] [Somatosensory Activation and Attentional States in Creative Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09960)
*Katherine Rees*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合舞蹈人类学、感官民族志与设计学的混合方法论，以具身方法记录创作过程，并探讨可穿戴技术激活体感系统、提升实践者感官意识的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录创作过程面临记忆回溯、表达沟通及记录方式的张力，现有方法难以捕捉具身的创造性实践，因此需要探索替代性的具身记录方法。

Method: 整合研究者作为参与者、田野笔记、具身知识/动作回溯、记谱形式与参与者访谈等舞蹈人类学既有方法，并结合探索性原型制作与工作坊方法，拓展为跨学科实践；同时通过可穿戴技术激活体感系统。

Result: 具身方法与原型制作、工作坊结合实现了跨学科拓展；可穿戴技术促进实践者感官意识的提升，使其能够聚焦于与反思和元认知相关的创作决策。

Conclusion: 该混合方法论为记录创造性制作提供了具身替代方案，并揭示了可穿戴技术通过增强体感意识支持创作反思与元认知的潜力。

Abstract: The methods for capturing the creative process come with associated tensions around memory recall, articulation, and communication during the act of making, as well as how to record these considerations. This paper has a twofold purpose: first, to offer an example of a mixed methodology, drawn from dance anthropology, sensory ethnography, and design, that applies embodied methods as an alternative for documenting creative making. Specifically, this incorporates the researcher-as-participant and the collation of fieldnotes, embodied knowledge/movement recall, with notation forms, and participant interviews. These are existing methods in dance anthropology; however, using them alongside exploratory prototyping and workshop approaches broadened this work into transdisciplinary practice. Second, it discusses the activation of somatosensory systems through wearable technology and the facilitation of heightened sensory awareness for the practitioner, leading to a subsequent ability to focus on creative decisions linked to reflection and metacognition.

</details>


### [13] [Streaming P300 Acquisition and Statistical Signal Validation Across Five EEG Platforms: A Hardware-Agnostic BrainFlow/LSL Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10047)
*Isabella Guan,Rui Liu,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一套基于BrainFlow与LSL的硬件无关实时P300采集管线，并在五种消费级/科研级EEG设备上验证，发现Emotiv Flex信号最具潜力，但整体P300仍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P300拼写器是重度运动障碍（如ALS）患者的重要无创沟通手段，语言模型虽提升了拼写器性能，但其底层EEG硬件却缺乏系统研究，硬件差异对信号可分离性与解码效果的影响尚不明确。

Method: 构建基于BrainFlow与LSL的硬件无关实时P300采集管线，配合置换检验评估信号可分离性；采用标准6×6行列范式，先试点五种配置（自制干电极、自制湿/凝胶、Emotiv Flex、Emotiv EPOC X、Muse 2），再对Flex开展20次会话，变化被试、时序与短语长度（131个目标字符），使用峰值幅度置换检验与交叉验证xDAWN解码器，并在两种通道排除策略下评估。

Result: 自制系统与EPOC X信号可分离性弱或不一致；Muse 2采集可靠性最高但中央顶区覆盖有限；Flex信号最有前景。Flex在20次会话中，两种通道策略下均检测到显著目标响应，15次重复后解码器AUC约0.72。字符准确率高度依赖评估方法：样本内多数投票达94.7%，而字符留出准确率仅31.3%（随重复累积证据），约为留出多数投票的三倍。

Conclusion: 在所研究条件下，Flex能采集到可检测但仍较弱的P300信号，未来仍需更大规模被试级验证与更优解码方法。

Abstract: P300 spellers offer people with severe motor impairment, such as ALS, an effective communication channel and remain one of the most established surgery-free alternatives to intracortical interfaces. Advanced language models have made spellers faster and more robust, yet the hardware beneath them is under-studied. We present a hardware-agnostic, real-time P300 acquisition pipeline built on BrainFlow and Lab Streaming Layer (LSL) that runs unchanged across consumer- and research-grade EEG headsets, with permutation tests of signal separability. Using a standard 6 x 6 row/column paradigm, we piloted five configurations: a custom dry system, a custom wet/gel system, Emotiv Flex, Emotiv EPOC X, and Muse 2. The custom systems and EPOC X showed weak or inconsistent signal separability, Muse 2 had the highest acquisition reliability despite limited centro-parietal coverage, and Flex showed the most promising signal. In 20 further Flex sessions varying subject, timing, and phrase length (131 target characters), a peak-amplitude permutation test and a cross-validated xDAWN decoder both detected a significant target response under two channel-exclusion policies, with decoder AUC reaching about 0.72 after 15 repetitions. Character accuracy depended heavily on evaluation methodology: in-sample majority voting reached 94.7%, whereas character-held-out accuracy was 31.3% with evidence accumulated across repetitions, about three times that of held-out majority voting. These analyses indicate that Flex captured a detectable, if still weak, P300 under the studied conditions, while broader participant-level validation and improved decoding remain necessary.

</details>


### [14] [Seeing the Voice, Preserving the Self: A Participatory Design Approach to Deaf-Centric Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10199)
*Shela Atemnkeng,Patrick Boudreault,Paige DeVries,Lloyd May,Christian Vogler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过参与式设计方法，与聋人及听障（DHH）参与者共同探索面向聋人中心的文本转语音（TTS）技术设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS在主stream发展迅速，但在聋人与听障技术领域关注不足，聋人用户面临无法通过非听觉方式操控语调、情感与表达，难以在不听的情况下验证语音是否匹配意图，以及需尊重文化与身份因素等关键问题未被解决。

Method: 采用参与式设计方法，开展两组焦点小组、三次协同设计会议、四次一对一早期设计评估会议；参与者包括熟悉与不熟悉TTS的人以及DHH内容创作者。

Result: 报告了关键发现、设计想法、结果及对未来聋人中心TTS开发的启示，并识别出阻碍聋人中心TTS技术采用的不满足的技术需求。

Conclusion: 聋人中心TTS需要解决非听觉操控、意图验证与文化身份尊重等问题，并满足现未被满足的技术需求以促进采用。

Abstract: We describe a participatory design approach toward developing Deaf-centric text-to-speech (TTS) technologies. While TTS is growing rapidly in the mainstream, it has received little attention to date in the deaf and hard of hearing (DHH) technology space. Critical problems have remained unaddressed for DHH users, including the ability to manipulate tone, emotions and delivery via non-auditory means. Verifying that the generated speech matches intent and is appropriate for a given situation without having to listen to it is another challenge. Respecting cultural and identity factors in the generated speech is also important. This work explores the design space with DHH participants through two focus groups, three co-design sessions, and four one-on-one early-stage design evaluation sessions. Participants included people both familiar and unfamiliar with TTS, as well as DHH content creators. We describe key findings, design ideas, results, and implications for future Deaf-centric TTS development. We also identify unmet technology requirements that pose barriers to adoption of Deaf-centric TTS technology.

</details>


### [15] [MOONWALK: Mediating Operations with Intent-Evidence-Action Alignment Across Junior-Supervisor Review Workflows in Animation/VFX Pre-Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10385)
*Shih-Yu Lai,Wen-Fan Wang,Sai Ling,Shaune Jan,Bing-Yu Chen,Xiang Anthony Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出"意图-证据-行动对齐"设计框架，并实现MOONWALK系统用于动画与VFX前期审片，通过结构化工作流提升意图对齐、决策可追溯性与清单可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动画与VFX前期审片中，创意意图表述松散（简报、演化中的规格、异构参考、口头决策），导致评判标准在迭代中漂移、审片判断失去证据支撑、请求背后的推理难以在资深-初级交接中保留。初级美术难以在无需反复澄清的情况下执行修改。

Method: 提出"意图-证据-行动对齐"设计框架：意图被明确为共享项目记录，判断锚定于有据可依的证据，授权决策转化为与参考笔记直接挂钩的清晰修改任务。将该框架实例化为MOONWALK系统，包含共享意图记录、参考/规格锚定、结构化在制品比较、主管授权的行动计划。AI仅承担行政协调（标记缺失上下文、组织笔记），艺术家保留全部创意主导权。在工作室中开展专业从业者研究，将MOONWALK与仅聊天的聊天机器人界面对比（匹配的制作素材），并将参与者现有工作流作为回顾性生态基线。

Result: 结果显示，MOONWALK在意图对齐、决策可追溯性和清单可执行性方面表现更强；同时也表明审美权威与最终优先级排序必须保留在从业者手中。评估确立了集成式结构化工作流优于非结构化对话式AI聊天机器人的价值。

Conclusion: 结构化、证据锚定且保留人类创意权威的工作流，能够有效解决动画与VFX前期审片中的意图漂移与交接损耗问题，优于纯对话式AI方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Animation and VFX pre-production review requires teams to translate loosely specified creative intent--briefs, evolving specifications, heterogeneous references, and verbal decisions--into revisions that junior artists can execute without repeated clarification. In practice, criteria drift across iterations, review judgments lose their evidential basis, and the reasoning behind a request rarely survives the senior-junior handoff. We contribute a design framework for intent-evidence-action alignment: intent is articulated into a shared project record, judgments are anchored to grounded evidence, and authorized decisions are converted into clear revision tasks tied directly to reference notes. We instantiate this framework in MOONWALK, a professional pre-production review system comprising a shared intent record, reference/specification anchoring, structured work-in-progress comparison, and supervisor-authorized action planning. In this workflow, AI handles administrative coordination--flagging missing context and organizing notes--while artists retain full creative direction. An in-studio study with professional practitioners compares MOONWALK with a chat-only (chatbot) interface using matched production materials, while participants' existing workflows provide a retrospective ecological baseline. Results indicate stronger intent alignment, decision traceability, and checklist executability, while also showing that aesthetic authority and final prioritization must remain with practitioners. The evaluation establishes the value of the integrated structured workflow over unstructured conversational AI chatbot. Code: https://github.com/Akinesia112/Moonwalk/tree/english-version

</details>

### [16] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集，包含558条完整AI科学智能体推理轨迹，通过记录每步9字段的过程性trace（思维、工具调用、观察、错误、修正触发、自信度）来评估科学方法学，而非仅看最终产出。初步分析显示，过程trace能揭示输出评估无法察觉的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI科学家基准仅评估最终产出（生成的代码、假设或论文），丢弃了获得这些产出所依赖的推理过程，导致无法审计科学方法学、诊断失败模式，也无法区分系统性推理与幸运猜测。因此需要一个记录完整推理过程的数据集来支持过程级评估。

Method: 构建OpenDiscoveryTrace公开数据集：558条AI科学智能体完整轨迹，每条记录每步的9字段结构化trace（含思维、工具调用、观察、错误、修正触发、自报自信度等）；覆盖124个科学任务，涉及药物发现、材料科学、基因组学和科学文献分析。涵盖7个模型：3个前沿模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro，各124条，跨领域和难度完全平衡）和4个开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5-1.5B，各30条），另有60条实时检索变体轨迹。定义5个基准任务，基线包括逻辑回归、随机森林、LSTM和Transformer模型。

Result: 对363条经LLM评判的轨迹进行试点分析发现，过程trace揭示了输出端评估无法察觉的行为差异：三个前沿模型成功率相近（84–89%），但Claude Opus 4.6产生的错误数比GPT-5.4多30倍（每条轨迹2.5 vs. 0.08，p < 0.0001，Cliff's δ = 0.613），且错误画像质上不同——Claude 66.7%为工具误用，GPT-5.4 83.6%为推理错误。

Conclusion: 过程trace能够暴露仅凭最终产出评估无法发现的行为与错误模式差异，为科学智能体的过程级评估、审计和AI治理提供支持。数据集、trace模式、智能体框架和基准定义均已以CC BY 4.0公开。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{OpenDiscoveryTrace}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>

*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率波框架，用广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程刻画自适应智能体的集体行为，并通过中国股市日内交易数据实证支持LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说，进而主张将自适应纠缠博弈模块引入AGI架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新古典金融基于独立理性主体的假设难以解释真实交易行为；同时，传统人工神经网络依赖海量不透明参数，缺乏对类人智能机制的可解释建模。作者希望通过可检验的本征模式与集体交易行为，间接检验LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说，并为AGI提供更具大脑启发性的模块。

Method: 提出概率波框架，通过广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程推导可检验的本征模式，刻画相互作用的适应型智能体的集体行为；并以中国股票市场日内数据为样本进行实证分析，区分自适应纠缠博弈模式、对日内新闻/事件/环境适应的行为模式（表现为双均衡态与参考点突变）以及纯独立模式。

Result: 实证显示，自适应纠缠博弈模式解释了82%–94%（总体89%）的观测决策模式，与新古典金融独立理性主体预测形成鲜明对比；2%–12%的行为表现出对日内新闻、事件和环境的适应，具有双均衡态与参考点突变特征；纯独立模式占比不足5%。这些结果被认为间接支持LCA假说。

Conclusion: 可观测交易行为反映大脑机制与行为心理学中的内部智能决策，支持LCA假说。研究强调将自适应纠缠博弈模块纳入AGI架构的必要性，以克服依赖万亿级不透明参数的传统ANN式AI的局限；通过将ANN式AI与基于概率波的纠缠大脑仿真结合，可丰富AGI基础模型，并推动利用类脑机制的人类式处理单元（HPU）发展，最终构建更紧凑、高效、鲁棒的AGI系统，尤其适用于具身智能与机器人领域。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [18] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将可复用技能包以子代理方式调用，比在主上下文中加载技能指令更适用于长时程任务，前提是技能包具备清晰的输入输出契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中，把技能指令加载进主代理上下文会导致上下文膨胀、推理质量下降、执行变得脆弱，因此需要探索技能包的组织与调用方式。

Method: 对比两种技能调用方式：一是把技能指令加载进主代理上下文（agent-skill execution），二是把技能包作为子代理调用，为其分配全新的上下文窗口来解子任务（subagent execution）。

Result: 当技能包暴露清晰的输入输出契约、且其指令编码了履行该契约所需的程序性知识时，子代理执行优于主上下文中的技能执行；代价是额外的通信开销，主代理与子代理协调需要消耗更多 token。

Conclusion: 可复用知识的收益不仅取决于知识内容本身，还取决于它如何被组织与调用。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [19] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验一个假设：物理交互的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比矩阵）刻画了现象体验的结构，并在理想化世界 Gradland 中通过两个雅可比结构度量（有效秩与凝聚度）对多种体验现象给出解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图为意识/现象体验的结构寻找一个基于物理交互一阶结构（梯度或雅可比）的刻画方式，并在物理规律已知、函数大多可微的理想化环境 Gradland 中对其进行检验。

Method: 构建理想化世界 Gradland（由神经网络居住，物理规律已知、函数大多可微）；提出基于 Kirchhoff 复杂度的两个雅可比结构度量——有效秩（effective rank）与凝聚度（cohesion）；并将这些度量应用于一系列实例分析。

Result: 该假设能够解释七类现象：(1) 体验可持续数百毫秒的时长；(2) 生动体验与模糊体验之别；(3) 质地体验；(4) 新生儿可能的"繁茂嘈杂的混乱"；(5) 清晰持有于心中的观念与混乱观念之别；(6) 学习是什么样子；(7) 丰富稠密体验的功能。

Conclusion: 物理交互的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比）可作为现象体验结构的有效刻画，两个基于 Kirchhoff 复杂度的雅可比度量在 Gradland 的实例分析中成功覆盖了多种体验特征，并解释了丰富稠密体验的功能。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [20] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个人类在环的多智能体GeoAI系统，用于北极生态航行，将运营、物理、生态与社区准则整合进统一路径规划框架，支持更安全、透明且负责任的北极航行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰条件变化扩大了北极航行的季节性可达性，但同时带来运营、环境与社区风险。北极航线规划本质上是多准则问题：提升安全性或效率的路线可能增加对海冰、敏感生态系统或邻近社区的暴露。现有路径规划方法侧重航行时间、燃料消耗与航行风险，往往忽视生态与社区影响。

Method: 提出人类在环（human-in-the-loop）的多智能体GeoAI系统。多个专门化智能体协同完成地理空间数据获取与准备、多目标路线生成，以及基于Skyline的决策支持。生态准则显式考虑对敏感区域的暴露，包括关键鱼类栖息地（Essential Fish Habitat）与海豹关键栖息地。

Result: 在统一框架中整合了运营、物理、生态与社区相关准则，并实现多目标路线生成与Skyline决策支持；项目页面与代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过考虑生态系统影响与潜在社区负担，同时将关键价值判断保留在人类控制之下，该框架可支持更安全、更透明且更具社会责任感的北极航行。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [21] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"状态路径"概念与State-Path Tool Menu框架，通过学习从请求状态到目标结果的预执行路线，为智能体构建有序工具菜单，显著提升多步任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际智能体面对包含数千接口的工具库，一次只能调用菜单中的工具。多步任务要求最终动作及其前置生产工具以可用顺序出现，而现有构造器仅按请求相关性排序，容易凸显最终动作却遗漏或延后不明显的前置工具。

Method: 将菜单视为状态路径上的执行先验。编码器表示：当前状态下哪些工具可运行、其输出如何满足后续输入、以及训练路径中反复出现的顺序。检索器覆盖可执行入口、缺失输入的生产者和最终动作；重排器将生产者置于消费者之前。

Result: 在ToolBench上，在线成功率从0.737提升至0.898，超过检索、重排、生成和路由等基线，且不改变智能体。用32个工具的菜单比官方128个工具的列表覆盖更完整链条，成功率增益在不同模型容量的执行器家族中持续存在。

Conclusion: 状态路径工具菜单能有效学习有序执行路线，以更少工具获得更高成功率，具有跨执行器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [22] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文分析材料回收中放大的恢复工艺选择问题，将实验室结果与产品需求、工艺成本和规模效应关联。基于CICERO自主选择性沉淀工作流，通过条件回顾性基准测试和主动学习，用更少实验达到最佳记录结果，并提出以贝叶斯风险缩减选择批次的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为放大的恢复工艺选择需要将实验室结果与产品需求、工艺成本和规模效应联系起来，因此需要更高效的实验设计策略来在有限实验次数下找到最优回收结果。

Method: 基于Pacific Northwest National Laboratory的CICERO自主选择性沉淀工作流，采用主动学习利用先前结果选择实验；进行条件回顾性基准测试，使用拟合模型和回收的NdFeB磁体记录；并开展条件分析（如SmCo磁体）与探索性模拟；提出按下游贝叶斯风险预期降低来选择批次，并对比混合筛选策略与实现的联合搜索。

Result: 在条件回顾性基准中，主动学习比非自适应空间填充用更少实验找到最佳记录结果；自适应策略在16至24个孔（实验）达到记录富集最大值，而基线为48；两阶段重建在16个孔处与两种自适应替代方案持平。SmCo磁体Round 2显示纯度与名义产率之间的权衡，NdFeB Round 1路线在富集上存在差异；油气采出水的排名取决于相态和稀释假设，需要确认。探索性模拟中，混合筛选候选方案比实现的跨路线和条件的联合搜索估计损失更低，但涉及合成两阶段政策的差异相对估计不确定性较小。

Conclusion: 提出在共享损失和记录标准下进行预注册的前瞻性测试，要求澄清测量和记录、定义工艺决策和相关输出、可靠的经济输入，并在预期规模上进行验证。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [23] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Valerant，一个无需训练即可将预训练的动作条件世界模型转化为世界动作模型（WAM）的框架，通过视觉预测与SLAM空间重建结合，将单张图像逐步构建为持久3D游戏地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）将预测式世界建模与动作生成耦合，可让预期未来状态引导智能体行为，在具身AI领域快速发展，但游戏领域的通用WAM仍未被充分探索。现有游戏方法多在2D视觉观察空间操作，缺乏持久3D几何结构。自动驾驶和机器人有独立于模型的持久3D物理世界，而游戏没有这种外部基底，虚拟世界必须被自身实例化；可玩3D游戏更需支持移动与交互的显式几何，而动作条件视频展开只能提供视觉观察而非空间表征。

Method: 提出 Valerant，一个无需训练的框架，将预训练的动作条件世界模型转化为用于探索和构建3D游戏地图的WAM。方法将预测性视觉展开与基于SLAM的空间重建以及探索驱动的动作选择相耦合，从单张图像出发逐步构建持久的3D游戏地图。

Result: Valerant 能够将单张图像逐步转化为持久的3D游戏地图，验证了将WAM式交互从2D视觉模拟扩展到3D映射构建的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架将基于WAM的交互扩展到2D视觉模拟之外，为减少3D游戏地图创建中的人工投入提供了新途径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive world modeling with action generation, allowing anticipated future states to guide agent behavior. Although WAMs are rapidly advancing embodied AI, general-purpose counterparts remain largely unexplored in games. Existing game-oriented approaches often combine action-conditioned world models with external policies and reward functions to realize WAM-like decision-making, yet they operate mainly in 2D visual observation space and do not instantiate persistent 3D geometry. Extending this paradigm to 3D games introduces a distinct challenge. In autonomous driving and robotics, the physical environment exists independently of the model, providing a persistent 3D world in which selected actions can be executed. Games have no such external substrate; the virtual world itself must be instantiated. Most playable games require a persistent and navigable space, while 3D games additionally require explicit geometry that supports movement and interaction. Action-conditioned video rollouts provide visual observations but not this spatial representation. We present \textsc{Valerant}, a training-free framework that transforms a pretrained action-conditioned world model into a WAM for exploring and constructing 3D game maps. By coupling predictive visual rollouts with SLAM-based spatial reconstruction and exploration-driven action selection, \textsc{Valerant} progressively transforms a single image into a persistent 3D game map. This framework extends WAM-based interaction beyond 2D visual simulation and offers a new approach to reducing manual effort in 3D game-map creation.

</details>


### [24] [XAI-Arena: Can LLMs Assess the Quality of XAI Explanations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09428)
*Yanfei Hu Fleischhauer,Alona Zharova,Nadja Klein,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 XAI-Arena，一个基于 LLM-as-a-judge 的框架，用于对可解释 AI（XAI）解释质量进行可扩展、可复现、多维度且面向利益相关者的评估，并通过人类验证证明其与人工评分具有强正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 XAI 解释质量评估方法多依赖主观人工判断，导致可复现性、可扩展性差，且不同研究之间难以比较。作者希望探索 LLM 能否作为可复现、可扩展的机制来对 XAI 解释质量进行比较性评估。

Method: 提出 XAI-Arena 框架，采用 LLM-as-a-judge 方式，从多个维度评估 XAI 解释质量，包括感知简洁性、清晰度、任务充分性、信任校准、可操作性、透明性、忠实性和整体可解释性；并在不同数据集、机器学习模型和利益相关者角色（personas）上对多种 XAI 解释方法进行基准测试。

Result: 人类验证显示 LLM 生成评分与人工评分之间存在强正相关（Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001），表明基于 LLM 的评估能够捕捉 XAI 解释质量的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 的评估可为 XAI 解释质量的比较性评估提供可扩展且可复现的框架，并有效捕捉解释质量的系统性差异。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality of explanations produced by explainable AI (XAI) methods remains challenging because existing approaches often rely on subjective human judgment, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and comparability between studies. We examine whether LLMs can serve as a reproducible and scalable mechanism to make comparative assessments of the quality of XAI explanations. We introduce XAI-Arena, an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable, reproducible, multidimensional, and stakeholder-sensitive evaluation of XAI explanation quality. XAI-Arena then allows us to compare XAI explanations along various dimensions, namely, perceived simplicity, clarity, task adequacy, trust calibration, actionability, transparency, faithfulness, and overall interpretability. We then benchmark XAI explanation methods across various datasets, machine learning models, and stakeholder personas. Human validation shows a strong positive association between LLM-generated and human ratings (Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001). Together, LLM-based evaluations can capture systematic differences in XAI explanation quality and provide a scalable and reproducible framework for comparative assessment of XAI explanations.

</details>


### [25] [Do Agents Know When They Succeed? Calibrating Agent Confidence from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09448)
*Priyanka Mary Mammen,Emil Joswin,Srujananjali Medicherla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多轮智能体任务中，模型内部表征是否能更强地预测任务成功，并提出LTD与ARP两种方法，在多个基准和模型上优于生成与序列校准基线，实现零开销可靠性监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统快速应用于安全关键场景，需要度量其动作置信度；相比传统机器学习，智能体工作流具有规划、工具调用和动态环境交互等复杂失败模式。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：Latent Trajectory Dynamics（LTD），汇总交互轨迹中残差流表征的变化；Action Representation Probe（ARP），基于动作决策处形成的表征预测成功。

Result: 在三个交互基准（Bash、SQL、Python）和三个模型系列（Qwen14B、Qwen7B、DeepSeek6.7B）上，方法持续优于表层生成和序列校准基线。

Conclusion: 模型内部表征可提供更强的多轮智能体任务成功信号，形成无需提示修改或多样本采样的零开销可靠性监测器。

Abstract: As agentic systems getting adopted rapidly in safety critical applications, it is vital to measure the confidence associated with the agentic actions. In comparison to the traditional machine learning systems, agentic workflows have complex failure modes with planning, tool invocation and dynamic environment interactions. In this paper, we investigate whether model's internal representations provide stronger signals of eventual task success in multi-turn agentic setups. We introduce two complementary methods: Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD), which summarizes changes in residual-stream representations across an an interaction trajectory, and the Action Representation Probe (ARP), which predicts success from representations formed at action decisions. Across three interactive benchmarks (Bash, SQL, Python) and three model families (Qwen14B, Qwen7B, DeepSeek6.7B), our methods consistently outperform surface level generation and sequence-based calibration baselines providing a zero-overhead reliability monitor that requires neither prompt alterations nor multi-sample rollouts.

</details>


### [26] [ContractEval: Query-Conditioned Execution Matching for Procedural Instruction Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09458)
*Praphul Singh,Shanu Kumar,Akshat Agarwal,Ganesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CONTRACTEVAL诊断框架，将程序性指令表示为"查询激活的义务"，并与回答或轨迹证据匹配，把遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、多余动作、不变量违反和输出契约违规区分为不同的符合性失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从回答问题转向执行流程时，失败可能"无据可查"而非明显错误：最终回答看似合理，但系统跳过了使答案成立的检查、分支、依赖或不变量。仅评估输出只看答案，轨迹感知评判只看活动，二者都无法识别哪些义务对该查询处于激活状态。

Method: CONTRACTEVAL将程序性指令表达为查询激活的义务，并将其与回答或轨迹证据进行匹配，从而将遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、多余动作、不变量违反、输出契约违规转化为可区分的符合性失败。在受控的经审计程序契约套件上进行评测，并采用金标准期望图与观测图，同时测试LLM驱动的提取方式。

Result: 仅输出与轨迹感知的LLM评判器会漏掉许多注入的结构性失败；在金标准期望图与观测图下，ContractEval能够检测并定位全部失败。LLM驱动的提取保留了大部分这一信号，但仍对校准敏感。

Conclusion: ContractEval并非合规保证，而是使程序性符合性变得可审计，而非隐式地依赖最终回答质量。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from answering questions to carrying out procedures, failures can be unwarranted rather than visibly wrong: the final response looks acceptable even though the system skipped the check, branch, dependency, or invariant that made the answer justified. Output-only evaluation sees the answer, and trace-aware judging sees activity, but neither identifies which obligations were active for the query. We introduce CONTRACTEVAL, a diagnostic framework for making those active obligations explicit. It represents procedural instructions as query-active obligations and matches them against response or trace evidence, turning omissions, wrong branches, ordering errors, extra actions, invariant breaches, and output-contract violations into distinct conformance failures. On a controlled suite of audited procedural contracts, output-only and trace-aware LLM judges miss many injected structural failures; under gold expected and observed graphs, ContractEval detects and localizes all of them. LLM-backed extraction preserves much of this signal but remains calibration-sensitive. ContractEval is therefore not a compliance guarantee; it makes procedural conformance auditable rather than implicit in final-answer quality.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09565)
*Liang Qu,Jianxin Li,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体图学习框架MAAGL，通过社区划分让各智能体专精不同区域，并分离结构与语义证据以解决排列不变性与上下文膨胀问题，在四个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体图学习（AGL）方法（单智能体或角色多智能体）在整个图上共用同一推理策略，对具有异构结构与语义模式的图而言是次优的。虽然多智能体协作是自然的补救思路，但直接应用于图面临两大挑战：一是将图结构语言化会破坏图固有的排列不变性；二是采样邻域扩大导致上下文迅速膨胀。

Method: MAAGL将图划分为若干社区，为每个社区分配一个独立智能体以实现区域特化；将结构与语义证据分开表示：结构证据用动态更新的、排列不变且定长的结构签名概括，语义证据则按相关性筛选出top-k节点；智能体基于相似签名的历史轨迹估计置信度，并在需要时触发辩论式协作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MAAGL的性能优于当前最先进的AGL方法。

Conclusion: 通过社区级智能体专业化、排列不变的结构签名与top-k语义筛选，以及基于置信度的辩论式协作，MAAGL有效缓解了排列敏感性与上下文膨胀问题，并在图推理任务上取得了优于SOTA的表现。

Abstract: Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

</details>


### [28] [CityPlanner: A Sandbox Agent for Executable Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09578)
*Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Zetong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Wenrui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CityPlanner——面向可执行城市规划的沙箱-智能体框架，并配以原子任务强化学习，在真实基准上超越启发式、任务专用 RL 与通用 LLM 智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规划是真实际空间优化问题，需在成本、服务质量等现实目标下从巨大候选空间中选出可行方案；现有优化与强化学习方法局限于固定问题形式，依赖任务专用表示与约束处理，泛化与可执行性不足。

Method: 构建 UrbanSandbox：统一、基于文件的环境，智能体可读取任务文件、生成方案、运行评估器，并依据可执行反馈迭代修订；提出原子任务强化学习，将长沙箱轨迹拆分为 BuildPlan（初始建造）与 ImprovePlan（反馈驱动改进）两个原子任务。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，CityPlanner 稳定优于启发式方法、任务专用强化学习方法与通用 LLM 智能体基线；消融实验验证了 UrbanSandbox、原子任务 RL 以及迭代部署的各自贡献。代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: CityPlanner 表明，将可执行沙箱环境与原子任务强化学习相结合，是提升城市规划等现实空间优化任务可行性与性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Urban planning is a real-world spatial optimization problem that requires selecting feasible actions from large candidate spaces under practical objectives such as cost and service quality. Existing optimization and reinforcement learning methods are effective for fixed formulations, but often depend on task-specific representations and constraint handling. We propose \emph{CityPlanner}, a sandbox-agent framework for executable urban planning. CityPlanner introduces \emph{UrbanSandbox}, a unified file-based environment where agents inspect task files, generate plans, run evaluators, and revise decisions based on executable feedback. To make learning tractable, we further propose atomic-task reinforcement learning, which decomposes long sandbox trajectories into \emph{BuildPlan} for initial construction and \emph{ImprovePlan} for feedback-based refinement. Experiments on a real-world benchmark show that CityPlanner consistently outperforms heuristic, task-specific RL, and general LLM-agent baselines. Ablations verify the contributions of UrbanSandbox, atomic-task RL, and iterative deployment. We release the code and dataset at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/co-agent-C1C8

</details>


### [29] [A Function-Space Approach to the Statistical Mechanics of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09589)
*Yizhou Zhang,Weichen Wu,Lun Du,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在函数空间中建立学习的统计力学描述，将参数配置视为微观实现、函数及其动力学算子视为宏观变量，推导出由学习算子 M=JJ* 控制的精确误差动力学，并通过对局部涨落积分得到涨落势 Φ_fluc(M;B)，揭示其旋转稳定性、特征值配对偏好及 ReLU 情形下 B 与结构曲率的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络参数空间高度非线性，却表现出规则的宏观行为。作者希望找到一个合适的宏观层面（函数空间）来统一描述学习中稳定的集体组织现象，从而弥合微观参数动力学与宏观规律之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用统计力学方法直接在函数空间展开：把参数配置当作微观实现，把函数及其动力学算子当作宏观变量。针对均方损失，用学习算子 M=JJ* 刻画精确误差动力学；结合条件随机动力学的动力学玻尔兹曼权重与参数空间态密度（其局部曲率定义统计算子 B），对局部涨落积分，得到涨落势 Φ_fluc(M;B)=σ_ξ²/2·logdet(M⁻¹+B)+const，并分析其旋转稳定性与特征值结构，进而对 ReLU 型函数空间推导 B 的具体形式。

Result: 在固定谱下，当 [M,B]=0 时该涨落项具有旋转稳定性；它被 M 的大特征值与 B 的小特征值配对所最小化，并对旋转失配产生局部回复贡献。对 ReLU 型函数空间，在温和的稳定统计条件下 B=σ_ξ²L*𝒦L，其中 L 度量粗粒化二阶结构。因此低 B 区域（在 𝒦 的有界各向异性意义下）对应低结构曲率，意味着偏好沿光滑、数据自适应方向更快弛豫。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了函数空间是研究学习中稳定集体组织的一个自然宏观层面。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit regular macroscopic behavior despite highly nonlinear dynamics in vast parameter spaces. We develop a statistical-mechanical description of learning directly in function space, treating parameter configurations as microscopic realizations and functions with their dynamical operators as macroscopic variables. For mean-squared loss, the exact error dynamics are governed by the learning operator \(M=JJ^\ast\). Combining the dynamical Boltzmann weight of the conditional stochastic dynamics with the parameter-space density of states, whose local curvature defines a statistical operator \(B\), and integrating over local fluctuations yields
  $$ Φ_{\mathrm{fluc}}(M;B)=\frac{σ_ξ^2}{2}\log\det(M^{-1}+B)+\mathrm{const}. $$
  At fixed spectrum, this term is rotationally stationary when \([M,B]=0\), is minimized by pairing large eigenvalues of \(M\) with small eigenvalues of \(B\), and generates a local restoring contribution against rotational mismatch. For ReLU-type function spaces under mild stable statistical conditions, \(B=σ_ξ^2L^\ast\mathcal K L\), where \(L\) measures coarse-grained second-order structure. Thus the low-\(B\) sector corresponds, up to bounded anisotropy of \(\mathcal K\), to low structural curvature, implying a preference for faster relaxation along smooth, data-adaptive directions. These results identify function space as a natural macroscopic level for studying stable collective organization in learning.

</details>


### [30] [From State Synchronization to Cognitive Self-Evolution: An Operational Architecture for Cognitive Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09625)
*Haoran Gao,An Li,Zhen Li,Jun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种包含物理层、数字孪生层、认知层与任务层的四层认知数字孪生（CDT）架构，构建跨层自演化闭环运行回路，并刻画用户请求驱动与自主驱动两种运行模式，最后通过轻量仿真验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDT研究多聚焦于学习模块、知识图谱、大语言模型等具体使能技术，缺乏对"认知如何系统性融入数字孪生架构"的整体性认识，因而难以指导CDT系统的设计与开发。

Method: 提出四层CDT架构（物理层、数字孪生层、认知层、任务层），建立四层之间的自演化闭环运行回路：物理状态同步为数字表征，认知通过知识、记忆与注意力构建任务特定认知模型，并在实际约束下生成任务级决策，运行反馈进一步优化认知经验并更新数字表征中的关系与标注。基于该框架刻画用户请求驱动认知与自主驱动认知两种运行模式，并讨论语义通信、知识查询、任务编排与闭环同步等关键使能机制与部署挑战。

Result: 轻量级仿真表明，在有限语义信息条件下闭环任务仍具备可行性与可靠性，且通过积累任务经验可提升运行效率。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来CDT系统的设计与开发提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: As Digital Twin (DT) systems evolve beyond state synchronization toward task-oriented and knowledge-driven operation, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) have emerged as an extension that incorporates cognitive capabilities into twin operation. Existing CDT studies often focus on specific enabling techniques, such as learning modules, knowledge graphs, and large language models, while providing limited insight into how cognition can be systematically integrated into DT architectures. To address this issue, this paper proposes a four-layer CDT architecture consisting of the physical layer, digital-twin layer, cognitive layer, and task layer. The proposed architecture establishes a self-evolving closed operational loop spanning these four layers, in which physical states are synchronized into digital representations, cognition constructs task-specific cognitive models through knowledge, memory, and attention, and task-level decisions are generated under practical constraints. Operational feedback further refines cognitive experience and updates relationships and annotations in the digital representation, enabling subsequent task interpretation, initiation, and reasoning to evolve with system operation. Based on this framework, two representative operation modes are characterized: user-request-driven cognition and self-driven cognition. We further discuss key enabling mechanisms and deployment challenges associated with semantic communication, knowledge querying, task orchestration, and closed-loop synchronization. A lightweight simulation study illustrates reliable closed-loop task feasibility under limited semantic information and improved operational efficiency through accumulated task experience. The proposed framework provides a structured foundation for the design and development of future CDT systems.

</details>


### [31] [Seven Sources of Physical AI Capability Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09627)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"能力形成来源"概念，归纳出七种非互斥来源（RE、PM、EI、SE、MG、EC、ED），并通过重构式归纳与理论饱和检验，用49条证据记录验证其可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按形态、架构、学习算法、任务或领域划分的分类法无法直接回答"能力由何而来"，且物理AI能力可能来自完全不同的形成历史，因此需要一个以能力形成为中心的分类框架。

Method: 采用重构式归纳与理论饱和方法：将研究矩阵追溯至原始研究，去重文献，设定编码规则，并进行三轮最大差异与负例抽样（R1–R3），涵盖课程与自监督学习、主动推理、开放式与发育学习、规划与搜索、神经符号架构、数字孪生、生成式物理世界模型、形态-控制协同设计等议题。

Result: 截至2026年9月4日既定范围内，全部49条证据记录均可由七种来源单独或组合解释；R1–R3未产生不可还原的第八种来源，R3也未引入新的核心定义或实质性边界规则。

Conclusion: 在既定范围内主张理论饱和（非逻辑完备或穷尽未来覆盖）。该框架区分"观测能力相似"与"形成方式相似"，可支撑对解释、迁移、复现、依赖关系、治理证据及地缘经济基础的分析。

Abstract: Capabilities relevant to Physical AI can arise from materially different formation histories, yet existing taxonomies organized by morphology, architecture, learning algorithm, task, or domain do not directly answer what gives rise to a capability. We define a capability-formation source as a factor materially contributing to capability formation, distinct from components or construction steps. We identify seven non-exclusive sources: Recorded-Experience (RE), Predictive-Modeling (PM), Evaluative-Interaction (EI), Surrogate-Environment (SE), Mechanism-Grounded (MG), Embodied-Coupling (EC), and Evolution-Driven (ED) Formation. Using reconstructive induction with theoretical saturation, we traced a research matrix to primary studies, deduplicated the literature, set coding rules, and conducted three rounds of maximum-difference and negative-case sampling. Challenges included curriculum and self-supervised learning, active inference, open-ended and developmental learning, planning and search, neuro-symbolic architectures, digital twins, generative physical world models, and morphology-control co-design. Within the scope and criteria fixed as of September 4, 2026, all 49 evidence records were explainable by the seven sources individually or in combination. No R1-R3 challenge produced an irreducible eighth source, and R3 required no new core definition or substantive boundary rule. We therefore claim theoretical saturation within the stated scope, not logical completeness or exhaustive future coverage. The framework distinguishes similarity in observed capability from similarity in how it was formed, supporting analysis of explanation, transfer, replication, dependencies, governance evidence, and geoeconomic foundations.

</details>


### [32] [RobustSGPO: Search-Space Control for Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09646)
*Zibo Zhao,Jijun Shi,Mo Zhou,Zhongyuan Wang,Shifu Bie,Yunfei Zhang,Xuanting Zhou,Xiangyu Wu,Bin Liu,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RobustSGPO，改进了基于语义梯度的提示优化（SGPO）：明确编辑请求、构造并检查补丁，并在当前解与保留快照间继续搜索；在 AgentX 头脑风暴流程中验证权限调度、累积控制与任务族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGPO 虽利用执行反馈改进智能体框架，但其局部更新规则未解决编辑范围与操作选择的问题，导致优化过程不稳定或不明确。

Method: RobustSGPO 显式指定请求的编辑，构造并检查补丁，然后从当前解或保留快照继续搜索；在 AgentX 头脑风暴工作流中评估周期性的 1→2→3 权限调度、累积控制及任务族迁移，实验规模为 120 个任务、95 次运行、7,350 次候选尝试。

Result: 周期性 1→2→3 调度比固定最大权限高出 0.28 测试分；RobustSGPO 在 30 个留出任务上将完成率从 60.0% 提升至 80.0%，在 2000 万 token 预算下测试质量从 3.77 提升至 4.14。类别保留可减少源任务迁移后的退化，而随机保留达到更高的目标端点。

Conclusion: 搜索空间控制通过可执行编辑和替代起点提升质量，但存在可测量的保留开销；RobustSGPO 在权限调度与迁移场景下优于基线。

Abstract: Semantic-gradient-based prompt optimization (SGPO) improves agent harnesses using execution feedback, but its local update rule leaves the choice of edit scope and operation unresolved. We introduce RobustSGPO, which specifies the requested edit, constructs and checks the patch, and continues search from either the incumbent or retained snapshots. We evaluate permission scheduling, cumulative controls, and task-family transfer in the AgentX brainstorming workflow using 120 tasks, 95 runs, and 7,350 candidate attempts. Periodic $1\to2\to3$ scheduling exceeds fixed maximum permission by 0.28 test-score points. RobustSGPO increases completion on 30 held-out tasks from 60.0% to 80.0% and improves test quality from 3.77 to 4.14 under a 20-million-token budget. Category retention reduces source-task degradation after a shift, whereas random retention reaches a higher destination endpoint. Search-space control benefits quality through executable edits and alternative starting points, with measurable retention overhead.

</details>


### [33] [Black-Box Red Teaming of Agentic AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Framework for Automated Risk Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09647)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Nitin Aravind Birur,Tanay Baswa,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需基本系统描述的黑盒、风险感知的智能体评估框架，通过七域风险分类、自动化红队工具SAGE-RT与人工验证的LLM评判，对CrewAI和AutoGen两类智能体架构进行评测，揭示出系统性的安全漏洞。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正快速进入生产环境，能够读取不受信任的输入、以真实权限调用工具并自主行动，使安全面远超仅对话模型。然而现有评估仍以单轮为主，无法捕捉多步智能体的漏洞，且往往依赖特权访问，难以规模化。

Method: 构建黑盒风险评估框架，仅需基本系统描述。包含三部分：（1）将可观测行为映射到风险类别的七域分类体系；（2）全自动红队工具SAGE-RT，每个域生成120个对抗性场景；（3）基于LLM评判并经过人工验证的评估方法。在CrewAI与AutoGen两种架构、四种基础模型上进行实证验证。

Result: 实验显示：平均治理风险达56.25%，多智能体配置下隐私风险达65%，智能体行为漏洞最高达85%，表明关键架构性漏洞普遍存在且严重。

Conclusion: 该黑盒方法无需特权访问即可有效识别关键架构漏洞，为更安全的智能体部署提供了可扩展的评估路径。

Abstract: Agentic systems are rapidly moving to production, where they read untrusted inputs, call tools with real permissions, and act autonomously, expanding the security surface beyond chat-only models. Yet standard evaluations remain single-turn and fail to capture multi-step agent vulnerabilities. We present a systematic black-box framework for risk-aware agent evaluation requiring only basic system descriptions. Our approach introduces: (1) a seven-domain taxonomy mapping observable behaviors to risk categories, (2) fully automated SAGE-RT red teaming producing 120 adversarial scenarios per domain, and (3) human-validated evaluation using LLM judges. Empirical validation across two agent architectures (CrewAI and AutoGen) with four base models reveals alarming patterns: 56.25\% average governance risk, 65\% privacy risk in multi-agent configurations, and agent behavior vulnerabilities reaching 85\%. Our black-box approach effectively identifies critical architectural vulnerabilities without privileged access, providing a scalable path toward safer agent deployments.

</details>

*Haichuan Hu,Yang Xiao,Mingni Tang,Jiawen Duan,Quanjun Zhang,Congqing He,Hao Zhang,Jiashuo Wang,Johan F. Hoorn,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系感知情感支持对话新任务，构建RESCUE基准（191个样本、7,079个标注轮次、1,064.8分钟视频），定义六项任务评估LLM的关系理解与关系敏感支持能力，发现当前模型在关系密集型任务上表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统主要关注一对一求助者-支持者交互和个体情绪状态，忽视了多 party 场景中的人际关系，导致关系感知的情感支持能力未被充分探索。

Method: 从真实的伴侣与家庭访谈对话中构建RESCUE基准，包含191个样本、7,079个标注轮次与1,064.8分钟视频；基于社会情感与支持动态的丰富标注，设计六项任务，覆盖关系理解（Relational Understanding）与关系敏感支持（Relation-Sensitive Support）两大核心能力；对十种LLM进行实验评估。

Result: 实验显示，当前LLM在依赖局部情绪或干预线索的任务上表现较好，但在关系模式预测、观点预测和支持策略预测等关系密集型任务上表现困难。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在人际关系的建模及关系敏感的支持决策方面存在明显局限，RESCUE为关系感知情感支持对话研究提供了新的评估基准与方向。

Abstract: Existing emotional support conversation systems mainly focus on one-on-one seeker-supporter interactions and individual emotional states, leaving interpersonal relations in multi-party scenarios underexplored. In this work, we introduce relation-aware emotional support conversation, a new task that evaluates whether LLMs can capture and utilize the evolving dynamics of relationships to offer more effective emotional support. We construct RESCUE (Relation-aware Emotional Support Conversation Understanding and Evaluation Benchmark) from real couple and family interview conversations, containing 191 samples, 7,079 annotated turns, and 1,064.8 minutes of video. Based on rich annotations of socio-emotional and support-related dynamics, RESCUE defines six tasks that evaluate two core capabilities required for relation-aware emotional support: Relational Understanding and Relation-Sensitive Support. Experiments with ten LLMs show that current models perform relatively well on tasks relying on local emotional or intervention cues, but struggle with relation-intensive tasks such as relation pattern prediction, viewpoint prediction, and support strategy prediction. These findings reveal the limitations of current LLMs in modeling interpersonal relations and making relation-sensitive support decisions.

</details>


### [35] [PRAGMA: Evaluating Personalized Guidance with Memory Alignment in Lifelong Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09664)
*Hyojeong Yu,Hyukhun Koh,Minsung Kim,Yunah Jang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRAGMA 基准，用于评估长期对话中的个性化指导能力，发现现有系统在检索对话证据及利用其进行个性化推理方面均存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 被越来越多地用作长期个性化助手，但长对话上下文带来计算开销大、信息利用不可靠等问题。现有对话记忆评估多聚焦检索与事实回忆，而现实中的个性化指导（推荐、规划、决策支持）需要跨多轮对话整合信息并推理用户偏好变化，这一能力尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建 PRAGMA 基准，包含精心整理的纵向对话历史、证据标注，以及基于用户情境演变和用户错误假设的指导场景，并对检索系统、记忆系统和长上下文模型进行实验评估。

Result: 实验表明，当前系统既难以准确恢复合适的对话证据，也难以有效地将其用于个性化指导。

Conclusion: 结果凸显出需要支持稳健对话检索和基于记忆推理的记忆架构，而不仅仅停留于证据回忆层面。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as personalized assistants that interact with users over extended periods of time. As conversations grow longer, relying on full interaction histories becomes increasingly inefficient and unreliable: long contexts introduce substantial computational overhead, making it difficult for models to consistently identify and utilize the most relevant information for the current request. These challenges have motivated memory systems that structure and retrieve user-specific information. In realistic interactions, users often seek practical guidance such as recommendations, planning, and decision support. Unlike factual recall tasks, personalized guidance requires models to integrate information across multiple past conversations and reason about changing user preferences and experiences. However, existing conversational memory evaluations mainly focus on retrieval and factual recall. To study this challenge, we introduce PRAGMA, a benchmark for evaluating personalized guidance in long-term conversations. PRGAMA contains curated longitudinal conversation histories, evidence annotations, and guidance scenarios grounded in evolving user contexts and incorrect user assumptions. Experiments across retrieval systems, memory systems, and long-context models reveal that current systems struggle both to recover the appropriate conversational evidence and to effectively use it for personalized guidance. Our results highlight the need for memory architectures that support robust conversational retrieval and memory-grounded reasoning beyond evidence recall.

</details>


### [36] [Safe to Stop? Risk-Constrained Stopping for Sequential Clinical Diagnosis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09678)
*Yuexin Wu,Vasile Rus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cros，一种风险受限的诊断停止层，通过状态误差排序、策略设计分离开发集与LTT精确检验，在MIMIC腹部疼痛基准上实现高覆盖率下的低选择性误差，但结果属探索性证据而非确证性安全认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诊断智能体基准多在固定或无约束交互后评估准确率，将自主停止的可靠性隐含处理，缺乏对"何时诊断或推迟"这一环节的显式风险约束与可靠评估。

Method: Cros结合状态级误差排序、在互斥开发划分上的策略设计，以及针对选择性诊断误差和完全序贯策略最低自主覆盖率的LTT风格精确检验；有限样本保证要求候选族、检验规则与随机化在访问校准标签前冻结。

Result: 在1,834回合MIMIC腹部疼痛基准上，完整排序器状态误差AUROC为0.853，优于最大类概率0.715和主干原生停止分0.552；在367回合评估划分上，对冻结权重解析平均得选择性误差16.9%、覆盖率78.8%、成本5.57、0.68次检测，原生停止则为30.8%误差、100%覆盖、成本8.14、1.53次检测。强制继续非单调：仅HPI时误差28.3%，全检查后34.3%。但均匀权重混合消融在已查看划分上更便宜且未满足锁定开发边际，Cros仅在20次开发重划分中的6次名义满足联合准则。

Conclusion: 由于评估标签在早期开发中被查看，这些发现仅提供探索性可行性与审计证据，不构成确证性安全证书。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis agents must decide not only what test to request next, but also when to diagnose or defer. Existing agent benchmarks largely evaluate accuracy after fixed or unconstrained interaction, leaving autonomous stopping reliability implicit. We present Cros, a risk-constrained stopping layer combining state-wise error ranking, policy design on disjoint development splits, and LTT-style exact tests of selective diagnostic error and minimum autonomous coverage for complete sequential policies. Its finite-sample guarantee requires the candidate family, testing rule, and any randomization to be frozen before calibration labels are accessed. On a 1,834-episode MIMIC-derived abdominal-pain benchmark, the full ranker achieves exploratory state-error AUROC 0.853, compared with 0.715 for maximum class probability and 0.552 for the backbone's native stop score. On the previously viewed 367-episode evaluation split, analytically averaging over the frozen Cros weights yields 16.9% selective error at 78.8% coverage, cost 5.57, and 0.68 tests, versus 30.8% error at 100% coverage, cost 8.14, and 1.53 tests under native stopping. Forced continuation is non-monotone: error is 28.3% with HPI alone and 34.3% after full workup. However, the uniform-weight mixture ablation is cheaper on this viewed split despite missing the locked development margins, and Cros nominally satisfies the joint criterion in only 6 of 20 development resplits. Because evaluation labels were inspected during earlier development, these findings provide exploratory feasibility and audit evidence, not a confirmatory safety certificate.

</details>
